    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:

        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
>               raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)

        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
>               raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
>               raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:

        cls,

        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,

        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
>               raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)

        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]

        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:

        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
>               raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
>               raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
>               raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
